# Filters filling the EMPTY cells of the type-separating lattice, over a window wider
# than the measured types occupy: A in [-0.02, 0.55], tau_L in [0.1, 0.45] s.
# Source: SNR_GeneralizedLattice_ClandininFilters.ipynb, section 5.
# Fit variant: UnconstrainedFit
# Lattice: 0.03330 (A) x 0.00895 (tau_L) s, offsets (+0.03293, +0.00415); cell (k, m)
#   spans [A0 + k dA, A0 + (k+1) dA) x [T0 + m dT, T0 + (m+1) dT).
# Polarity division: A* = 0.166130, the lattice line k = 4.
#   k <  K: fitted in the double-polarity rho' box (-3.0, -0.8).
#   k >= K: fitted in the single-polarity box (1.5, 3.0).
#   A* is the lattice line nearest the middle of the empty band between the two measured
#   clouds (0.0933 | 0.2492, middle 0.1712); it misclassifies none
#   of the 330 per-type filters and none of the 11 mean fits.
# Draw: 10 targets per cell, normal about the cell centre with sd
#   (0.0061, 0.0025 s) -- the dispersion the 30-per-type ensembles
#   realized, so each cloud has a measured cloud's spread -- clipped at 2.08 sd and
#   inflated by 1.168 to undo the clip. Seed 11.
# Fit: the inverse fit of Filters_ClandininData_Inverse.ipynb section 3, anchored on the
#   neighbouring cell's solution (reached by marching outward from the 11 occupied cells)
#   and STARTED at a forward sample of the rho' box screened for a positive dominant lobe
#   and, where one exists, a single positive peak. Without that screen 18% of the
#   double-polarity fits land on the branch dominated by their NEGATIVE lobe, which no
#   measured filter is and which the project's sign-alignment convention forbids.
# 130 of the 702 cells in the window are absent: their centre
#   is outside what the rho' box can produce (large area with a short half-life -- the
#   forbidden region). The 11 cells the measured types own are also absent; those keep
#   their 30 filters in GeneratedFilters_Inverse_*.txt.
#
# pos_dom / npk are the ACHIEVED waveform shape: is the dominant lobe positive, and how
# many positive peaks. Every measured mean fit is (1, 1). npk = 2 marks the diagonal band
# where the three-parameter family cannot do better: reaching that (A, tau_L) needs
# comparable weight on both gamma terms, and that is two humps. Filter on npk == 1 to
# restrict any analysis to fly-like waveforms.
#
# NOTE -- as in the inverse notebook, the per-cell spread is set by type IDENTIFIABILITY,
# not measured from the data; it is not an estimate of biological variability.
#
# Features on t = arange(0.001, 5, 0.001).
